TL;DR: 本文利用作用量形式体系，导出了描述自旋活性界面（至少一侧为扩散区）的准经典格林函数边界条件，并将该边界条件应用于反常邻近效应、自旋劈裂超导体与三重态超导体构成的约瑟夫森结等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自旋活性界面边界条件在数学上不够简洁、不易处理，且难以统一描述正常态与超导态下的电荷、热和自旋输运及其全计数统计。为此，作者希望借助作用量形式建立一组清晰、易用且普适的边界条件。

Method: 作者采用 Keldysh-Nambu-自旋形式的作用量形式体系，推导出自旋活性界面的准经典格林函数边界条件。该形式同时适用于正常态与超导态，并能导出电荷流、热流和自旋流的全计数统计。随后将所得边界条件用于反常邻近效应，以及磁界面耦合三重态超导体与自旋劈裂超导体的约瑟夫森结研究。

Result: 作者得到了 p 波超导体与自旋过滤器结的零偏压电导的解析表达式，并揭示了其对交换场与 d 矢量相对取向的依赖。在约瑟夫森结方面，证实了时间反演对称性所要求的结论：对于两个自旋劈裂超导体、以及自旋劈裂超导体与三重态超导体的组合，出现反常约瑟夫森电流所需的自旋相对取向条件明显不同。此外，只有当自旋向上和自旋向下两种透射率均非零且不相等时，反常约瑟夫森电流才伴随二极管效应。

Conclusion: 本文通过作用量形式建立了描述自旋活性界面的普适、简洁边界条件，能够统一处理正常态与超导态下的多种输运现象及其全计数统计，并在反常邻近效应和约瑟夫森结等具体物理问题中给出了可检验的解析预言，深化了对自旋、磁性与超导相互作用的理解。

Abstract: We present boundary conditions for the quasiclassical Green's functions describing spin active interfaces between two materials, at least one of which is in the diffusive regime. These boundary conditions are developed with the help of the action formalism, and provide clean and easily tractable expressions. We connect our expressions to previous literature in limiting cases and describe normal transport, magneto-resistance transport and spin-precession through the bound ary. By using the Keldysh-Nambu-spin formalism, our equations are valid both in the normal and superconducting state. The construction through the action formalism also allows us to derive the corresponding full counting statistics for charge currents, thermal currents and spin currents. We use this boundary condition to consider the anomalous proximity effect, and to study the Josephson coupling between triplet and spin-split superconductors through a magnetic interface. We find an analytic expression for the zero bias conductance in junctions with a p-wave superconductor and a spin-filter, and show how it depends on the relative orientation of the exchange field and d-vector. For Josephson junctions, we show that, in agreement with time-reversal symmetry, the conditions on the relative orientations of the spins in the materials for the appearance of anomalous Josephson currents, are distinctly different for two spin-split superconductors and a spin-split superconductor with a triplet superconductor. Moreover, we show that the anomalous Josephson currents are only accompanied by a diode effect if both transparencies for spin up and down are nonzero and distinct.

</details>

### [89] [Ground-state properties of the two-dimensional SWAP spin-glass ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05046)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Tarzia*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究Ising自旋玻璃的SWAP系综，通过退火交换自旋长度，在足够慢的退火下能以高概率获得基态；研究发现在中等/较大长度尺度（如Δ=1）时体系趋于铁磁而失去自旋玻璃性质，而Δ较小时体系保持玻璃性但SWAP退火难以找到真正基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将用于玻璃态多分散硬球系统的SWAP算法思路引入Ising自旋玻璃，利用可变自旋长度的交换机制配合退火来高效寻找自旋玻璃的基态，并检验该方法获得的无序实现是否仍保持零温自旋玻璃特性。

Method: 引入SWAP系综：自旋具有可变长度，长度尺度Δ∈[0,2]，可在SWAP算法框架下交换；将自旋长度并入键中形成对应Ising哈密顿量，采用退火策略求基态。通过与图匹配算法得到的精确基态进行对比验证，覆盖最大L²=1024²的二维系统；并利用周期性与反周期性边界条件下的精确基态计算零温畴壁能，以分析所得无序实现的本质。

Result: 对于二维、最大1024²自旋的系统，该方法在足够慢退火下能以高概率获得基态（经图匹配精确基态验证）。但对所得无序实现的分析表明：当Δ取中等或较大值（如Δ=1）且退火足够慢时，实现趋于铁磁，失去零温自旋玻璃性质；当Δ很小时实现保持玻璃性，但SWAP退火难以找到真正的基态。

Conclusion: SWAP系综退火只能在参数区间内有效找基态，但无法同时兼顾"高效找到基态"与"保持自旋玻璃性质"：大Δ下虽易找基态却使体系铁磁化，小Δ下虽保持玻璃性却难以找到基态，表明该方法在自旋玻璃基态研究中存在根本性局限。

Abstract: We study the recently introduced SWAP ensemble for Ising spin-glasses, where the spins obtain varying lengths, with length scale $Δ\in [0, 2]$. The lengths can be exchanged in the spirit of the SWAP algorithm for glassy poly-disperse hard-sphere systems. Using an annealing schedule, if the annealing is slow enough, ground states of the corresponding Ising Hamiltonian, where the spin lengths are incorporated into the bonds, can be obtained with high probability. We prove this statement for two-dimensional systems with sizes of up to $L^2=1024^2$ spins by comparison with exact ground states obtained by using graph matching algorithms. In particular, we analyze the nature of the obtained realizations of the disorder by calculating exact zero-temperature domain-wall energies, from exact ground state calculations using periodic and anti-periodic boundary conditions. For medium or large values of $Δ$, e.g. $Δ=1$, the realizations turn ferromagnetic if the annealing is slow enough, i.e., they lose their zero-temperature spin-glass property. If $Δ$ is small, the realizations remain glassy, but the SWAP annealing does not easily find a true ground state.

</details>


### [90] [Disorder-driven loss of crystalline coherence in two-dimensional electron solids: Quantum Hartree-Fock and classical molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06371)
*Sankar Das Sarma,Haining Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用Hartree-Fock理论与经典分子动力学，研究淬灭无序对二维电子固体的影响，发现无序削弱晶体相干性、降低相干长度，同时提高粒子重排温度、稳定非晶固相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究淬灭无序如何影响二维电子固体的结构有序性及其热稳定性，理解无序在破坏晶体相干性的同时如何影响非晶固体的动力学行为。

Method: 结合Hartree-Fock平均场理论与经典分子动力学模拟，通过结构因子、相干长度等表征晶体关联，并考察不同无序强度下的粒子重排温度。

Result: 无序增强会削弱晶体关联、展宽结构因子，使测量的相干长度趋近于原始晶格间距，符合Imry-Ma与Larkin图像；无序破坏长程平移有序，且无序越强，晶体组织可存续的尺度越小。经典模拟进一步表明，即使晶体序已消失，更强的钉扎仍会提高粒子重排温度。

Conclusion: 无序抑制结构相干性，同时在晶体序消失后仍通过钉扎稳定非晶固体、抑制热致重排，呈现"破坏晶体序却增强非晶热稳定性"的双重效应。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of quenched disorder on two-dimensional electron solids using Hartree-Fock theory and classical molecular dynamics. Increasing disorder weakens crystalline correlations, broadens the structure factor, and reduces the measured coherence length toward the pristine lattice spacing. This evolution is consistent with the Imry-Ma and Larkin picture: random pinning disrupts translational order on sufficiently long scales, and stronger disorder reduces the distance over which crystalline organization survives. Classical simulations further show that stronger pinning raises the temperature for particle rearrangement even after crystalline order has disappeared. Disorder thus suppresses structural coherence while stabilizing an amorphous solid against thermal rearrangement.

</details>

### [91] [Uniform meandric systems as percolation on planar maps: conjectures and a numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03730)
*David Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过将均匀蜿蜒系统(meandric systems)编码为一对简单随机游走，并将其解释为随机平面图上的临界渗流模型，进行数值研究；论文考察了盒交叉事件，给出相反开放交叉之间强负相关的数值证据并提出渐近猜想，同时引入一种基于局部峰/谷翻转的保律动力学，模拟表明渗流结构的解相关速度远快于底层随机平面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀蜿蜒系统兼具随机游走编码与平面图渗流两种视角，但其全局几何与交叉事件之间的相关性结构尚不清楚，缺乏解析工具；作者希望通过数值手段探索盒交叉事件间的相关性，并设计能高效混合的动力学以支持蒙特卡洛模拟。

Method: 作者将蜿蜒系统编码为一对简单随机游走，并解释为随机平面图上的临界渗流；针对特定盒交叉事件进行数值研究，估计其渐近行为并检验相反开放交叉间的负相关性；同时构造一种保分布(保律)的局部动力学，通过游走编码中的峰(peak)/谷(valley)翻转实现对蜿蜒系统构型的更新，并利用该动力学进行蒙特卡洛模拟以研究解相关时间。

Result: 数值证据表明，相反方向的开放交叉事件之间存在强负相关；作者据此提出关于其渐近行为的若干猜想。此外，模拟显示所提出的峰/谷翻转动力学保持蜿蜒系统的分布不变，且渗流结构的自相关/解相关速度显著快于底层随机平面图结构的混合(解相关)速度。

Conclusion: 均匀蜿蜒系统的盒交叉事件展现出与临界渗流一致而又具强负相关的非平凡性质，相关的渐近猜想有待严格证明；所提出的保律峰/谷翻转动力学为在该模型上高效采样与估计提供了实用工具，并揭示渗流层与平面图层在弛豫时间尺度上的分离。

Abstract: We perform a numerical study of uniform meandric systems via their encoding by pairs of simple random walks and their interpretation as a model of critical percolation on random planar maps. We investigate certain natural box-crossing events and provide numerical evidence for a strong negative association between opposite open crossings and formulate conjectures on their asymptotic behavior. Our second contribution is a simple law-preserving dynamics on meandric systems, by local peak/valley flips in the walk encoding. Our simulations suggest that the percolation structure decorrelates much faster than the underlying random planar map.

</details>


### [92] [Harnessing Measurement Error as a Thermodynamic Resource in Two-Reservoir Information Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03778)
*Yang Xiao,Qian Zeng,Jin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一类两热库信息引擎（TRIE），发现测量误差与功输出、效率及功涨落之间的单调关系由恶魔的控制参数决定，并给出在任意测量误差下产生有用功的条件，以及效率可超越卡诺界限的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为测量误差会限制信息引擎性能，但这一结论可能并非普遍成立。作者旨在探索测量误差并不必然降低性能的机制，并揭示控制参数如何决定测量误差与性能指标之间的单调关系。

Method: 提出一类由二能级系统构成的两热库信息引擎（TRIE），分析测量误差对功输出、效率及功涨落的影响，并推导由恶魔控制参数决定其单调关系的条件。进而设计双奥托循环 TRIE，并给出基于囚禁 40Ca+ 离子的实验实现方案。

Result: 证明了测量误差与功输出、效率、功涨落之间的单调关系由恶魔的控制参数决定；建立了在任意测量误差水平下 TRIE 仍能产生有用功的条件，并识别出可用测量误差同时优化这些性能参数的区间。当恶魔的能量成本仅由冷库提供时，计入该成本的效率可超越标准卡诺界限而不违反热力学第二定律。结果可推广至多能级系统信息引擎。

Conclusion: 测量误差对信息引擎性能的制约并非普适，通过选择适当控制参数可实现任意误差下的有用功输出、多性能参数的同时优化，以及超越卡诺界限的效率，且该结论可推广至多能级系统。

Abstract: In general, measurement error constrains the performance of information engines. However, this may not hold universally. In this work, we propose a class of two-reservoirs information engines (TRIEs) with a two-level system and demonstrate that the monotonic relationships between the work output, the efficiency, as well as the work fluctuations of the TRIEs and measurement error are determined by the demon's control parameters. Utilizing this, we establish the conditions that allow the TRIEs to generate useful work under any level of measurement error, and identify the regimes in which these performance parameters can be optimized simultaneously by the measurement error. Furthermore, we demonstrate that when the demon's energy cost is supplied solely by the cold reservoir, the efficiency that accounts for this cost can surpass the standard Carnot bound without violating the second law. Finally, we design a dual Otto cycles TRIE and provide an experimental scheme for its implementation based on a trapped $^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^+$ ion. Significantly, our results can be applied to the multi-level system information engines.

</details>


### [93] [Multicatalyst reactions induce abrupt transition in a dense catalytic reaction network model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04843)
*Kota Mitsumoto,Shuji Ishihara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将 Furusawa–Kaneko 模型推广到多催化剂反应，并用动力学平均场理论进行分析，揭示了网络异质性与催化剂数量对代谢相变和双稳态行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞内催化反应网络常表现出双稳态与突然切换等复杂代谢动态，但现有 Furusawa–Kaneko 模型过于简化（单催化剂），难以刻画多催化剂协同反应下的相变行为与网络异质性效应。

Method: 将 Furusawa–Kaneko 模型推广为多催化剂反应模型，在热力学极限与稠密网络极限下，采用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）推导精确有效方程，并针对任意度分布分析其不动点及分岔结构。

Result: 在营养匮乏条件下，网络异质性抑制代谢–饥饿相变，且与每反应催化剂数量无关；在营养丰富条件下，分岔结构随催化剂数量改变：单催化剂时仅出现连续相变，两个以上催化剂产生双稳态相与不连续相变；两个与三个催化剂的分岔结构也不同，三个催化剂允许不同细胞生长速率的代谢态之间出现双稳态。

Conclusion: 研究结果为代谢动态中的双稳态与突然切换提供了一种可能的机制，说明多催化剂反应与网络异质性共同塑造了代谢系统的非线性动力学行为。

Abstract: We generalize the Furusawa--Kaneko model, a simple model of intracellular catalytic reaction networks, to multicatalyst reactions and analyze the generalized model using dynamical mean-field theory. In the thermodynamic and dense-network limit, we derive exact effective equations and analyze their fixed points for arbitrary degree distributions. Under nutrient-poor conditions, network heterogeneity suppresses the metabolic--starvation transition irrespective of the number of catalysts per reaction. In contrast, under nutrient-rich conditions, the bifurcation structure changes with the number of catalysts. With one catalyst per reaction, only continuous transitions occur, whereas two or more catalysts give rise to bistable phases and discontinuous transitions. The bifurcation structure also differs between two and three catalysts per reaction, with three catalysts allowing bistability between metabolic states with different cell growth rates. Our results provide a possible mechanism for bistability and abrupt switching in metabolic dynamics.

</details>


### [94] [Information Entropy and Dissipation Potential in Stochastic Nambu Nonequilibrium Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03790)
*So Katagiri,Tatsuaki Wada*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究随机Nambu非平衡热力学中耗散势S_NB与信息熵的关系，证明当Nambu流保持耗散势且满足Einstein型关系时，稳态分布由e^{-S_NB}权重决定，故S_NB应视为稳态准势而非Shannon熵本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清随机Nambu非平衡热力学中耗散势S_NB的统计含义，厘清它与信息熵（Shannon熵）及热力学熵的关系，避免将耗散势误认为熵本身。

Method: 从纯扩散、梯度流及含Hamilton旋转流的系统出发，推导含Nambu流的Fokker-Planck方程的稳态分布；基于Nambu流保持耗散势与Einstein型条件证明指数权重；并以开放气-活塞系统（耦合热库与外压库）为例，利用热力学熵与耗散势的区分来阐释S_NB的Massieu解释，引入Nambu正则分布并在鞍点近似下计算涨落的协方差。

Result: 证明当Nambu流保持耗散势且扩散矩阵与输运张量满足Einstein型关系时，稳态分布由exp(-S_NB)决定，局部自信息与耗散势直接相关；S_NB被识别为刻画非平衡稳态分布的稳态准势，而非Shannon熵本身；得到Nambu正则分布及涨落协方差的鞍点近似表达式。

Conclusion: S_NB不应等同于Shannon熵或热力学熵，而应理解为非平衡稳态背后的稳态准势；这一统计解释为随机Nambu非平衡热力学的耗散势赋予了明确的概率意义。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between the dissipation potential $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ and information entropy in stochastic Nambu nonequilibrium thermodynamics (NNET). Starting from pure diffusion, gradient-flow systems, and systems with Hamiltonian rotational currents, we derive the stationary distribution of the Fokker--Planck equation including a Nambu flow. We show that, when the Nambu flow preserves the dissipation potential and an Einstein-type relation holds between the diffusion matrix and the transport tensor, the stationary distribution is governed by an exponential weight determined by $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$. Consequently, the local self-information is directly related to the dissipation potential, demonstrating that $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ should not be identified with the Shannon entropy itself but instead serves as the stationary quasipotential governing the nonequilibrium steady-state distribution. Using the distinction between the thermodynamic entropy and the dissipation potential established for an open gas--piston system coupled to a thermal bath and an external-pressure reservoir, we further clarify the Massieu interpretation of $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$, introduce a Nambu canonical distribution, and derive the covariance of fluctuations within the saddle-point approximation. These results provide a statistical interpretation of the NNET dissipation potential by identifying it as the stationary quasipotential underlying nonequilibrium steady states.

</details>


### [95] [Parisi's solution of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model from synchronous Monte Carlo dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05557)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把 Sompolinsky-Zippelius 用连续时间软自旋弛豫动力学推导 SK 模型 Parisi 解的做法推广到离散时间、全自旋同步更新的同步蒙特卡洛（SyncMC）动力学，证明其长时结构在相同假设下重现 Parisi 方程，并通过数值模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sompolinsky 和 Zippelius 曾借助连续时间软自旋弛豫动力学、假设时间尺度广泛分离而得到 SK 模型的 Parisi 解。本文旨在于离散时间、所有 Ising 自旋同时更新的同步蒙特卡洛动力学框架下完成同样的推导，从而将该动力学理论推广到离散时间情形。

Method: 引入高斯辅助场使自旋条件独立，SyncMC 对任意对称两体耦合系统精确采样 Boltzmann 分布；证明 SyncMC 在平衡态满足离散时间下的精确涨落-耗散关系（FDR）；对 SK 模型建立 SyncMC 的动力学平均场方程，其中相比连续时间动力学多出两项——每个自旋的自耦合以及经耦合关联的噪声，二者只影响快弛豫。在 Sompolinsky-Zippelius 相同假设（时间尺度广泛分离、每个慢时间尺度以约化比值 x 满足同样关系）下，导出长时结构重现 Parisi 方程（包括 Parisi 偏微分方程和局域场分布方程）。数值上用 up to N=2048 的副本交换平衡模拟排除实际局域场的副本对称分布；由重叠分布及 N=4096 随机初态弛豫响应估计的函数 x(q) 也与 Parisi 结果一致。

Result: SyncMC 动力学的长时结构在宽分离时间尺度假设下重现包括 Parisi 偏微分方程和局域场分布方程在内的完整 Parisi 方程；数值模拟表明实际局域场分布非副本对称（拒绝 RS 分布并支持 Parisi 解），从重叠分布和弛豫响应估计的 x(q) 亦与 Parisi 理论一致。

Conclusion: 离散时间同步蒙特卡洛动力学在平衡态满足离散时间精确 FDR，其动力学平均场方程虽新增自耦合项和相关噪声项（仅影响快弛豫），但在 Sompolinsky-Zippelius 假设下长时行为严格重现 Parisi 方程；数值证据（副本交换模拟与 x(q) 估计）进一步支持 Parisi 解，完成了离散时间情形下的对应推导。

Abstract: Sompolinsky and Zippelius obtained Parisi's solution of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) model from the relaxational dynamics of soft spins in continuous time, by assuming a hierarchy of widely separated time scales. Here we carry out the same programme for a discrete-time dynamics in which all Ising spins are updated at once. This dynamics, synchronous Monte Carlo (SyncMC), introduces Gaussian auxiliary fields so that the spins become conditionally independent, and it samples the Boltzmann distribution exactly for any system with symmetric pairwise couplings. We show that, for any such system, the correlation and response functions of SyncMC obey an exact fluctuation-dissipation relation in discrete time in equilibrium. The dynamical mean-field equations of SyncMC for the SK model contain two terms absent in continuous-time dynamics, a self-coupling of each spin and a noise correlated through the couplings; both affect only the fast relaxation. As a result, under the same assumptions as in the Sompolinsky-Zippelius construction (widely separated time scales, and on each slow scale the same relation with a reduced ratio x, as expected in relaxation from random initial conditions), the long-time structure of SyncMC reproduces the Parisi equations, including the Parisi partial differential equation and the equation for the distribution of local fields. Equilibrium simulations with replica exchange for up to N=2048 spins exclude the replica-symmetric distribution of the actual local fields and are consistent with the Parisi solution. The function x(q) estimated from the overlap distribution, and from the response in relaxation from random configurations for up to N=4096 spins, is also consistent with Parisi's.

</details>


### [96] [An Absorbing State Model with an Emergent Ising Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03936)
*Shen Ai,Dov Levine,Paul Chaikin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定在方格晶格上的排斥性针状粒子动力学模型，发现其相变边界具有与二维伊辛模型相同的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非平衡吸收态相变的普适性，特别是系统自组织形成的棋盘对称相在熔化过程中的临界行为是否属于已知的普适类，旨在理解排斥性针状粒子模型在远离平衡条件下的相变特性。

Method: 建立排斥性针的动力学模型：针中心固定在方格晶格上，重叠的针为活跃态并受到 θ∈[0,ε) 的排斥旋转，无重叠的针保持静止。通过改变针长和 ε 参数，分析系统从棋盘对称有序相到无序相（吸收态相变）的熔化过程，并计算相变临界指数。

Result: 模型表现出吸收态相变；长针排列呈现向列序。在一定针长和 ε 范围内，系统自组织成棋盘对称态。当 ε 增大时，该相熔化并变为无序。相边界处测得的临界指数与平衡态二维伊辛模型完全一致。

Conclusion: 该非平衡排斥性针模型的棋盘相熔化相变与二维伊辛模型属于同一普适类，表明这一远离平衡的动力学系统在相变层面上表现出与平衡统计力学系统的深刻类比。

Abstract: We study a dynamical model of repulsive needles with centers fixed on a square lattice. Needles that overlap are active and receive a repulsive rotation $ θ\in [0,ε)$; needles with no overlap do not move. This model exhibits an absorbing state transition. Nematic order was observed for long needles. For a range of needle lengths and $ε$, the system self-organizes into a state with checkerboard symmetry. As $ε$ is increased, this phase melts and becomes disordered. Remarkably, we find that this phase boundary exhibits critical exponents identical to those of the equilibrium 2D Ising model.

</details>


### [97] [Transport, Hall Effect, and Collective Dynamics of Driven Particles in Active Chiral Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03977)
*C. Reichhardt,C. J. O. Reichhardt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了直流驱动粒子穿越手性活性粒子（作圆周运动）体系的输运行为，发现随驱动强度出现堵塞相、非线性塑性相（存在霍尔效应）和高驱动线性相（相分离、霍尔效应接近消失）三种动力学相，并绘制了相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手性活性粒子的圆周运动与热涨落如何影响被直流驱动的、具有Yukawa相互作用的粒子的输运特性，尤其是非平衡体系中的霍尔（横向）效应与动力学相变。

Method: 采用模拟方法，考察具有Yukawa相互作用的粒子在直流驱动下穿过由手性活性粒子（作圆周运动）组成的组装体的输运过程，通过改变活性轨道尺寸、热涨落和粒子密度等参数，分析纵向与横向输运曲线并确定不同动力学相。

Result: 被驱动粒子呈现三种相：1）堵塞相；2）非线性无序/塑性相，此时与手性活性粒子相互作用产生有限霍尔效应；3）高驱动线性相，驱动与非驱动粒子发生相分离，霍尔效应几乎消失。手性粒子受到被驱动粒子的强拖曳与霍尔效应，且这些效应随热涨落或手性运动参数变化而单调变化。

Conclusion: 不同动力学相与纵向和横向输运曲线的特征相关联，作者以活性轨道尺寸、热涨落和粒子密度为参量绘制了完整相图，揭示了手性活性粒子体系中驱动输运与霍尔效应的调控规律。

Abstract: We examine the dc driven transport of particles with Yukawa interactions through an assembly of chiral active particles undergoing circular motion. The driven particles exhibit a jammed phase, a nonlinear disordered or plastic regime where there is a finite Hall effect produced by interactions with the active chiral particles, and a high drive linear regime where the driven and nondriven particles phase separate and the Hall effect nearly disappears. The chiral particles experience strong drag and Hall effects from the driven particles, and these effects vary monotonically with changing thermal fluctuations or chiral motion parameters. The different dynamical phases are correlated with features in the longitudinal and transverse transport curves, and we map out the phases as a function of active orbit size, thermal fluctuations, and particle density.

</details>


### [98] [Casimir Force, Magnetization, and Susceptibility for \\ Ising Chain with an Impurity Atom in the Presence of an External Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04035)
*Daniel Dantchev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了含单个杂质原子的 N 原子伊辛链的卡西米尔力的精确结果，考察力随 N、温度 T 及外磁场 h 的变化，发现力可为吸引或排斥，取决于杂质耦合比 J_ia/J 的符号与大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究杂质原子对一维伊辛铁磁链中卡西米尔力的影响机制，特别是杂质耦合常数的符号（铁磁或反铁磁）与大小如何决定力的性质。

Method: 对含一个杂质原子的 N 原子伊辛链建立模型，仅考虑最近邻铁磁相互作用 J>0，杂质与近邻以 J_ia 耦合（可为铁磁或反铁磁），通过精确求解得到卡西米尔力关于 N、温度 T 和外磁场 h 的表达式。

Result: 得到卡西米尔力行为的精确结果；力既可表现为吸引力也可表现为排斥力，具体取决于杂质耦合比 J_ia/J 的符号和大小。

Conclusion: 杂质原子通过其耦合常数的符号与强度显著改变链中卡西米尔力的方向与大小，表明可通过调节杂质耦合实现卡西米尔力从吸引到排斥的调控。

Abstract: The article contains exact results for the behavior of the Casimir force of Ising chain of $N$ atoms containing an impurity atom. The force is considered as a function of $N$ and on both the temperature $T$ and the external magnetic field $h$. The interactions between nearest neighbors within the chain are supposed to be of a ferromagnetic short-ranged type $J>0$. The impurity atom interacts with constants $J_{ia}$ with its nearest neighbors, where $J_{ia}$ can be both of ferromagnetic type, i.e., $J_{ia}>0$, as well as of antiferromagnetic type, i.e., $J_{ia}<0$. It turns out that the Casimir force can be both attractive or repulsive, depending on the sign and the magnitude of the ratio $J_{ia}/J$.

</details>


### [99] [From Equilibrium Criticality to Universal Collective Phases through Nonreciprocal Coupling of Ordered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04450)
*Fabrizio Rippa,Jacopo A. Garofalo,Eugenio Lippiello*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自旋系统间非互易耦合所驱动的非平衡集体行为，发现除已知的振荡（交换）相外，还出现多稳态等更丰富的非平衡现象，并证明这些现象可追溯到孤立模型的临界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用已成为远离平衡态集体行为的基本驱动力，但已有研究主要关注振荡（swap）相。作者旨在探索孤立动力学偏向有序态的自旋系统在非互易耦合下是否会产生更丰富的非平衡现象，并揭示这些现象与孤立模型临界性质的深层联系。

Method: 作者以孤立动力学趋向有序态的自旋系统为出发点，引入非互易耦合，分析其非平衡相图。利用平均场理论刻画多稳态、磁滞与硬激发等行为，并进一步超越平均场，推导出适用于有限维度下非互易振荡起始（onset）的普适场方程，从而建立连接临界现象、动力系统与非互易集体动力学的统一框架。

Result: 研究发现：非互易耦合会产生比振荡相更丰富的非平衡现象，包括有序态与无序态共存的多稳态区（平衡态下严格禁止）。孤立系统中的三临界性（tricriticality）导致振荡动力学中出现多稳态、磁滞和硬激发；非正规（non-normal）相互作用通过生成多稳态进一步丰富相图。在有限维度下，作者推导出描述非互易振荡起始的普适场方程。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了连接临界现象、动力系统理论与非互易集体动力学的统一理论框架，并指出其可应用于从观点动力学到活性物质等多个领域。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions have emerged as a fundamental driver of collective behavior far from equilibrium. Here, we show that nonreciprocal couplings between spin systems whose isolated dynamics favors ordered states, beyond the previously identified oscillatory (swap) phase, give rise to a richer nonequilibrium phenomenology, including a multistable regime where ordered and disordered states coexist, a scenario strictly forbidden in equilibrium. Crucially, we show that this nonequilibrium phenomenology is inherited directly from the critical properties of the underlying isolated model. In particular, tricriticality in the isolated system gives rise to multistability, hysteresis, and hard excitations in the oscillatory dynamics, while non-normal interactions further enrich the phase diagram by generating multistability. Moving beyond mean-field theory, we derive a universal field equation governing the onset of these nonreciprocal oscillations in finite dimensions. Our results establish a unified theoretical framework connecting critical phenomena, dynamical systems, and non-reciprocal collective dynamics, with applications ranging from opinion dynamics to active matter

</details>


### [100] [MUTACO: Simulator-Efficient Response Matching in Stochastic Nonlinear Systems via Compatibility-Aware Sequential Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04619)
*Ege Karadeniz,İlter Onat Korkmaz,Umut Can Turhan,Cem Tekin,Aydın Cem Keser*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出 MUTACO，一种仅需含噪声正向评估、无需梯度的不确定性感知序贯设计方法，用于在昂贵随机模拟器上实现响应匹配式反问题建模，并在随机相位振荡器无序网络上验证其模拟器效率与反演精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理反问题依赖昂贵的随机模拟器，但往往无法获得响应梯度、伴随灵敏度或可微模拟器接口；同时，实验可观测量通常刻画的是系综统计而非特定微观实现，因此需要一种仅利用有限噪声正向评估、以"响应兼容"而非"参数唯一恢复"为目标的高效反演方法。

Method: 提出 Multiple Target-based Confident Acquisition (MUTACO)，一种面向响应匹配的不确定性感知序贯设计方法。它仅需噪声正向评估，同时学习多分量响应及其预测不确定性，并搜索预测响应与目标响应在考虑预测不确定性下兼容的参数构型。在随机相位振荡器的无序网络上开展验证，反演任务是从多驱动条件下的宏观旋转与同步曲线推断局部势与相互作用的统计性质。

Result: 在严格受限的模拟预算下，MUTACO 在所测试的自适应搜索、空间填充及基于模拟的推断方法中取得最低的留出响应误差，且参数恢复误差也低于对比方法。

Conclusion: 面向响应的序贯设计是一种模拟器高效的反问题建模途径，适用于仅有有限噪声正向评估的情形；该方法对具有相关多分量响应和弱约束参数方向的随机物理系统具有更广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: Many physical inverse problems are mediated by expensive stochastic simulators for which response gradients, adjoint sensitivities, or a differentiable simulator interface are unavailable. We introduce Multiple Target-based Confident Acquisition (MUTACO), an uncertainty-aware sequential-design method for response matching that requires only noisy forward evaluations and learns the multicomponent response together with its predictive uncertainty. We develop and evaluate MUTACO for a disordered network of stochastic phase oscillators. The inverse task is to infer statistical properties of the local potential and interactions from macroscopic rotation and synchronization curves under multiple driving conditions. Rather than requiring unique recovery of these statistical properties, MUTACO searches for parameter configurations whose predicted rotation and synchronization responses are compatible with the target response while accounting for predictive uncertainty. This response-centered formulation is appropriate when experimentally accessible observables characterize ensemble statistics rather than a particular microscopic realization. Under strictly limited simulation budgets, MUTACO achieves the lowest held-out response error among the tested adaptive-search, space-filling, and simulation-based inference methods, while also yielding lower parameter-recovery error than the compared methods. These results demonstrate that response-targeted sequential design is a simulator-efficient approach to inverse modeling from finite noisy forward evaluations and suggest broader applicability to stochastic physical systems with correlated multicomponent responses and weakly constrained parameter directions.

</details>


### [101] [Active lattice percolation: an apparently new universality class of percolation transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04807)
*Vernon M. Hughes,David A. Huse*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究格点上的一类动态渗流模型，其中连接的增删遵循局域随机规则，且禁止任何会导致单一连通团簇分裂成多个团簇的连接移除。作者数值证明这类模型中的相变是混合渗流相变（HPT），兼具连续渗流的发散临界团簇与一阶相变的不连续性；在移除吸收态后相变趋于连续，但临界指数保持稳定，可能构成新的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于对 toric code 局域量子纠错模型的研究（Chirame 等，PRX Quantum 6, 030363 (2025)），该模型表现出双稳态和序参量不连续等一阶相变特征。作者希望厘清该相变的真实本质——即它究竟是一阶、连续还是兼具二者特征的混合渗流相变，并探究吸收态和几何各向异性在其中的作用。

Method: 作者在格点上定义一类受局域随机规则驱动的动态渗流模型，核心约束为“禁止任何会令单个团簇分裂为两个或更多团簇的连接移除”。通过数值模拟，作者：(1) 检验相变是否具有发散的临界团簇（连续特征）与不连续的序参量（一阶特征），从而判定其为混合渗流相变；(2) 将模型修改为完全各向同性以检验 HPT 是否保持；(3) 通过开启额外的各向同性局域随机移动来移除吸收态，观察一阶特征是否被抑制或消除；(4) 对比各模型的渗流临界指数，判断是否属于已知普适类。

Result: 数值结果表明：(1) 原始模型的相变确为混合渗流相变，兼具发散临界团簇与序参量不连续等一阶特征；(2) 将模型改为完全各向同性后 HPT 依然存在；(3) 在前两个模型中 HPT 一侧的稳态为吸收态；开启额外的各向同性局域随机移动移除吸收态后，一阶相变被强烈抑制甚至消除，留下表观连续的渗流相变；(4) 无论是混合相变模型还是表观连续相变模型，数值观测到的渗流临界指数均无显著变化，且与所有已知渗流相变显著不同，暗示这可能是一个新的普适类。

Conclusion: 作者确认了该类模型中的相变是混合渗流相变，且该 HPT 在完全各向同性情形下依然稳健；吸收态的存在是产生一阶（不连续）特征的关键，去除吸收态会使相变趋于表观连续。值得注意的是，相变的临界指数对模型修改（包括移除吸收态、改变各向异性）保持稳健，且不同于所有已知渗流普适类，提示可能发现了一个新的渗流普适类。

Abstract: We investigate a class of dynamic percolation models on a lattice, in which connections are added and removed by a set of local stochastic rules. In particular, the removal of any connection that can result in one connected cluster becoming two (or more) separate clusters is strictly forbidden. These models include and are inspired by a model for local quantum error correction for a toric code studied by Chirame, et al., PRX Quantum 6, 030363 (2025), who identified a phase transition with first-order characteristics, such as bistability and a discontinuous order parameter. We show numerically that this transition is a hybrid percolation transition (HPT), having characteristics of a continuous percolation transition such as diverging critical clusters while at the same transition having discontinuities like a first-order transition. We then modify this model to be fully isotropic, showing that the HPT remains. In both of these models, the steady state on one side of the HPT is an absorbing state; by turning on additional isotropic local stochastic moves, we remove the absorbing state and strongly suppress or remove the first-order transition, leaving an apparently continuous percolation transition. Curiously, the numerically-observed percolation critical exponents do not change significantly between the models with hybrid transitions and the model with an apparently continuous transition. These exponents are significantly different from known percolation transitions, indicating that this may be a new universality class.

</details>


### [102] [Confined Riesz gas: From Newtonian Dynamics to Emergent Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06626)
*Indranil Mukherjee,Abhishek Dhar,Manas Kulkarni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文结合大规模牛顿分子动力学模拟与三场（密度、速度、温度）流体力学框架，定量验证了Riesz气体中微观动力学与宏观流体力学演化在宽时间尺度上的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riesz气体的平衡性质已被广泛研究，但其微观动力学、宏观演化与热化之间的联系仍基本未被探索。作者旨在从第一性原理出发，在幂律相互作用系统中建立牛顿动力学与连续介质理论之间的具体桥梁。

Method: 采用大规模牛顿分子动力学模拟，结合以密度、速度、温度三个粗粒化场表述的流体力学框架。该理论引入两个唯象输运系数——体黏度ξ和热导率κ来刻画宏观耗散效应，并对穹顶型和牛顿摆型等典型初始条件类别进行分析。

Result: 在从较早阶段直至达到稳态的宽广时间尺度范围内，微观模拟与流体力学演化之间表现出极好的一致性；且这种一致性在ξ和κ的较大取值窗口内持续成立，表明涌现流体力学描述的稳健性。

Conclusion: 研究结果系统地验证了Riesz气体中流体力学的有效性，并从第一性原理出发，为具有幂律相互作用的系统在牛顿动力学与连续介质理论之间建立了具体桥梁。

Abstract: We investigate the real-time dynamics of the Riesz gas (RG), a paradigmatic system of particles with power-law interactions that encapsulates several many-body interacting classical systems. While the equilibrium properties of the system have been extensively studied the connection between microscopic dynamics, macroscopic evolution and thermalization has remained largely unexplored. Combining extensive large-scale Newtonian simulations with a hydrodynamic framework formulated in terms of three coarse grained fields -- density, velocity, and temperature -- we establish a direct and quantitative correspondence between the two descriptions. The hydrodynamic theory incorporates two phenomenological transport coefficients, the bulk viscosity $ξ$ and thermal conductivity $κ$, which encode dissipative effects at a macroscopic level. By analyzing representative classes of initial conditions, such as dome-like and Newton-cradle-type profiles, we find excellent agreement between microscopic and hydrodynamic evolution over a broad range of timescales, starting from relatively early all the way up to times at which steady state is attained. Notably, this agreement persists across a substantial window of $ξ$ and $κ$, demonstrating the robustness of the emergent hydrodynamic description. Our results provide a systematic validation of hydrodynamics in the RG and establish a concrete bridge between Newtonian dynamics and continuum theories in systems with power-law interactions, starting from first principles.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [103] [Time-dependent integrable quantum field theories with dynamical boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03870)
*Pradip Kattel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从自治可积模型的散射与反射数据出发，在有限空间区间上构造含时（驱动）可积手征量子场论的方法。核心结论是：谱标签沿自由轨迹为常数，且右-左散射事件对位置无关这一要求迫使特征坐标到谱标签的映射为仿射映射（两种手征斜率相同），从而同一时刻的所有体散射共享一个公共谱参数，两个端点反射则采样对应端点参数；体与边界的驱动并非独立变形，而是由同一仿射谱坐标固定。连续端点反射使谱标签平移有限量，完整往返给出差异输运，其相容性即边界量子 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov（qKZ）方程。作者进一步通过反解一、二粒子匹配问题重构局域哈密顿量，并以有理 SU(N) 模型和带动力学边界杂质的 O(3) 不变模型为例，得到其耦合由同一仿射谱坐标固定的驱动 Kondo 相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究含时可积量子场论通常因缺少对易守恒量而困难。作者希望利用自治可积模型的因子化散射/反射数据作为“种子”，系统生成时间依赖的可积边界驱动模型，并揭示体驱动与边界驱动之间的内在联系（二者并非独立）。

Method: 从自治可积模型的散射与反射数据出发构造含时手征场论：(1) 令每个粒子携带沿自由轨迹不变的谱标签；(2) 要求右-左散射事件在固定时刻与位置无关，推出特征坐标到谱标签为斜率相同的仿射映射，故同一时刻所有体散射共享一个公共谱参数，两端点反射采样对应端点参数；(3) 连续两次端点反射使谱标签平移有限量，完整往返构成差异输运，其相容性给出边界 qKZ 方程；(4) Yang–Baxter 与反射方程保证局域相容，qKZ 平坦性保证完整往返相容；(5) 反解一、二粒子匹配问题重构局域哈密顿量：体矩阵确定接触相互作用，反射矩阵确定边界耦合；(6) 解出边界 qKZ 系统后，其特征拉回给出含时多体波函数。

Result: 建立了一套完整构造框架：同一套自治因子化数据同时确定边界 qKZ 输运问题与局域驱动哈密顿量；体散射与边界反射的时间依赖性由同一仿射谱坐标统一固定，二者不是独立形变。在这套框架下，得到了有理 SU(N) 模型，以及 O(3) 不变、带动力学边界杂质模型中的驱动 Kondo 相互作用，其后者的耦合也由同一仿射谱坐标确定。

Conclusion: 作者提出并验证了一种利用自治可积模型因子化散射/反射数据系统构造含时可积（驱动）手征量子场论的方法。其核心几何约束——特征坐标到谱标签的仿射映射（两重手征斜率相同）——将体散射与边界反射的驱动统一到同一谱参数上，并通过边界 qKZ 方程给出完整往返的相容条件。该方法可具体落实到有理 SU(N) 与带动力学边界杂质的 O(3) 模型，产生由仿射谱坐标固定的驱动 Kondo 相互作用，展示了该框架在生成非自治可积模型方面的通用性。

Abstract: We present a method for constructing time-dependent integrable chiral quantum field theories on a finite spatial interval from the scattering and reflection data of an autonomous integrable model. Each particle carries a spectral label that is constant along its free trajectory. Requiring a right--left scattering event at a fixed time to be independent of its position forces the map from characteristic coordinates to spectral labels to be affine, with the same slope for both chiralities. As a result, all bulk scattering events at a given time sample one common spectral argument, and the two boundary reflections sample the corresponding endpoint arguments. The bulk and boundary drives are not independent time-dependent deformations: their time dependence is fixed by the same affine spectral coordinate. Successive reflections at the two endpoints translate the spectral label by a finite amount. Following a particle through a complete round trip then produces a difference transport whose consistency gives the boundary quantum Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov equations. The Yang--Baxter and reflection equations ensure local compatibility, while BqKZ flatness ensures compatibility of complete round trips. We then reconstruct the local Hamiltonian by inverting the one- and two-particle matching problems. The bulk matrix fixes the contact interaction, while the reflection matrix fixes the corresponding boundary coupling. The same autonomous factorized data determine the boundary qKZ transport problem and the local driven Hamiltonian. Once the boundary qKZ system is solved, its characteristic pullback gives the corresponding time-dependent many-body wavefunction. We illustrate the construction with rational $SU(N)$ models and an $O(3)$-invariant model with dynamical boundary impurities, obtaining driven Kondo interactions whose couplings are fixed by the same affine spectral coordinate.

</details>


### [104] [Quenched Growth of Normal Matrices and Harmonic Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03912)
*Oleg Alekseev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大正态随机矩阵特征值构成的二维库仑气体：在固定权重下，当矩阵秩增大时谱液滴随之增长，其半经典密度增量表现为调和测度。作者冻结一个 N 特征值构型，再利用在冻结点处消失的次数小于 N+M 的多项式投影来采样 M 个新特征值，证明其条件分布是冻结电荷势场中的一个精确多项式系综。对正则解析液滴，当 M/log N→∞ 且 M/N→0 时，新特征值的空间分布收敛到旧边界上的调和测度，且该收敛在冻结谱的条件下成立（除去概率趋于零的冻结构型集）。文章还给出精确有限秩恒等式，将条件均值的多项式矩与冻结谱的涨落联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大正态随机矩阵在秩增长过程中特征值（二维库仑气体）的增量分布规律，特别是新增特征值如何聚集在已有谱液滴的边界上，并刻画其收敛到调和测度的机制。

Method: 采用冻结 N 特征值构型、通过"在冻结点处消失的次数小于 N+M 的多项式投影"采样 M 个新特征值的构造方法；证明其条件分布是冻结电荷势的精确多项式系综；利用正交多项式方法构造平衡过程；并借助精确有限秩恒等式建立条件均值多项式矩与冻结谱涨落之间的关系。

Result: 对正则解析液滴，当 M/log N→∞ 且 M/N→0 时，新特征值的空间分布条件收敛于旧边界上的调和测度；该收敛对冻结谱条件成立，除一个概率趋于零的冻结构型集合外；由次数 N,…,N+M−1 的正交多项式构造的平衡过程具有相同极限。

Conclusion: 新增特征值的空间分布在恰当的标度区间内收敛到旧谱液滴边界上的调和测度，且此结果以高概率在冻结构型上条件成立；正交多项式平衡过程给出相同的极限，精确有限秩恒等式揭示了条件均值与冻结谱涨落之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The eigenvalues of a large normal random matrix form a two-dimensional Coulomb gas. At fixed weight, the spectral droplet grows with the matrix rank, and its semiclassical density increment is harmonic measure. We freeze an $N$-eigenvalue configuration and sample $M$ further eigenvalues using the projection onto polynomials of degree less than $N+M$ that vanish at the frozen points. Their conditional law is an exact polynomial ensemble in the potential of the frozen charges. For a regular analytic droplet, we prove that the spatial distribution of the new eigenvalues converges to harmonic measure on the old boundary when $M/\log N\to\infty$ and $M/N\to0$. This convergence holds conditionally on the frozen spectrum, outside a set of frozen configurations whose probability tends to zero. The equilibrium process formed from orthogonal polynomials of degrees $N,\ldots,N+M-1$ has the same limit. An exact finite-rank identity relates polynomial moments of the conditional mean to fluctuations of the frozen spectrum, with a remainder from polynomial modes of degree $N+M$ and above.

</details>


### [105] [Branching Gaps, Coexistence, and Boundary Influence for the Ising Model on Spherically Symmetric Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03941)
*Farrukh Mukhamedov,Otabek Khakimov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究根式球对称树（每顶点有1或q个子节点）上铁磁最近邻Ising模型：非分支长段导致唯一性，但未必有均匀速率；有界间隔下与周期树等价并给出共存区间；周期间隔下利用负Schwarz导数的返回映射实现完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非齐次分支结构（分支层序列中非分支的一子节点延伸段的长度与分布）如何影响铁磁Ising模型的相变、Gibbs唯一性以及有限体积边界影响的衰减速率。

Method: 构造根式球对称树上分支层序列，量化非分支延伸段的长度与位置；通过有限体积边界影响的衰减速率刻画唯一性命中效应；对有界间隔与周期树进行均匀比较；对周期间隔，引入全周期返回映射的不动点分析，并利用负Schwarz导数对映射进行完整分类。

Result: 充分长的无分支延伸段使任意非零均匀场、任意有限逆温度下唯一；唯一性不蕴含均匀速率，可构造密度为一、分支数仍为q、但非零场共存区域消失且边界影响沿子列更慢衰减的树；有界间隔下得到与最大允许间隔周期树的最优均匀比较和共存场显式区间；超临界情形返回映射在闭共存区间内有三个不动点，两个位于端点、一个在外部，非零端点自动为非退化折叠，合并分支呈平方根分裂。

Conclusion: 在该树族内，有界间隔等价于某有限温度与非零场下存在共存；周期间隔下Gibbs唯一性等价于全周期返回映射的不动点唯一性，并通过负Schwarz导数给出完整相变分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the ferromagnetic nearest-neighbor Ising model on rooted spherically symmetric trees with one or \(q\) children per vertex, according to a prescribed sequence of branching levels. Unbounded runs of one-child levels force uniqueness at every nonzero homogeneous field and every finite inverse temperature. We quantify this effect through the locations of sufficiently long nonbranching stretches. Conversely, uniqueness need not entail any uniform rate: for each prescribed vanishing sequence, we construct a density-one tree whose finite-volume boundary influence decays more slowly along a subsequence. The tree retains branching number \(q\), although its nonzero-field coexistence region disappears. For eventually bounded gaps, we prove an optimal uniform comparison with the periodic tree having the largest allowed gap and obtain an explicit interval of coexistence fields. Thus bounded gaps are equivalent, within this family, to coexistence at some finite temperature and nonzero field. For eventually periodic gaps, Gibbs uniqueness is equivalent to fixed-point uniqueness of the full-period return map. A negative Schwarzian derivative yields a complete classification: in the supercritical regime the map has three fixed points inside a closed coexistence interval, two at its endpoints, and one outside. The nonzero endpoints are automatically nondegenerate folds, with square-root splitting of the merging branches.

</details>


### [106] [A Self-Generated Local Structural Prior for Resolving Target Merging in Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04394)
*Junkang Liu,Yizhuang Song*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自生成局部结构先验方法，用于抑制电阻抗断层成像（EIT）重建中相邻目标因低分辨率而被合并的问题，并通过数值与水箱实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EIT 是一种病态逆成像技术，分辨率有限且空间分布不均匀；位于灵敏度退化区域的相邻电导率目标往往被重建为单一连通区域，导致空间可分性丢失，因此需要一种方法恢复邻近结构之间的可分性。

Method: 首先用现有 EIT 重建方法从边界测量数据获得初始电导率图像；以此重建结果作为参考图像，依据像素间强度相似性构造高斯相似矩阵；自适应选取协方差矩阵的特征向量以提取参考图像的主结构信息；据此构建局部化结构掩模，并将其纳入后续 EIT 重建以保持相邻目标的空间可分性。

Result: 数值仿真与水槽实验结果表明，所提方法能够有效分离被传统 EIT 重建方法合并的相邻电导率目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的自生成局部结构先验可有效抑制 EIT 重建中的目标合并现象，显著改善相邻电导率目标的空间可分性。

Abstract: Electrical impedance tomography (EIT) is an ill-posed inverse imaging technique with limited and spatially nonuniform resolution. Conductivity targets located in regions with degraded spatial sensitivity may be reconstructed as a single connected region, resulting in the loss of spatial separability between neighboring structures. In this work, we propose a self-generated local structural prior for suppressing target merging in EIT reconstruction. First, an initial conductivity image is obtained from the measured boundary data using an existing EIT reconstruction method. This reconstruction is subsequently used as a reference image to construct a Gaussian similarity matrix according to the intensity similarity between image pixels. The adaptively chosen eigenvector of the covariance matrix is then employed to extract the principal structural information of the reference image. A localized structural mask is constructed and incorporated into a subsequent EIT reconstruction to preserve the spatial separability of neighboring targets. Numerical and water tank experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively separates neighboring conductivity targets that are merged by conventional EIT reconstruction methods.

</details>


### [107] [Supersymmetric semi--infinite cohomology and $N = 2$ superconformal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04434)
*Thomas Creutzig,Uhi Rinn Suh,Sangwon Yoon*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将超仿射顶点代数上的 N=3/2 超共形结构进行 BRST 约化，研究超对称半无限上同调及其 N=2 超共形结构，并利用相对超对称半无限上同调实现超陪集模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超对称半无限上同调及其 N=2 超共形结构，并希望将超陪集模型纳入统一的 BRST 约化框架中加以理解。

Method: 文章基于满足 Kazama–Suzuki N=2 条件的超 Manin 对，构造超仿射顶点代数上的 N=3/2 超共形结构，并对其施行 BRST 约化，从而得到（相对）超对称半无限上同调并分析其 N=2 超共形结构。

Result: 得到了超对称半无限上同调上的 N=2 超共形结构，并证明超陪集模型可通过相对超对称半无限上同调实现。

Conclusion: BRST 约化方法成功将 N=3/2 超共形结构约化到超对称半无限上同调上，为超陪集模型提供了自然的实现方式。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology and its $N = 2$ superconformal structure via the BRST reduction of an $N = {\frac{3}{2}}$ superconformal structure on the super-affine vertex algebra associated to a super Manin pair arising from the Kazama--Suzuki $N = 2$ condition. Moreover, we realize super coset models in terms of relative supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology.

</details>


### [108] [Covariant Euler-Poincaré Reduction for Field Theories on Centered Semidirect Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04628)
*Miguel Ángel Berbel,Leonardo Colombo,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对结构群为中心半直积 G⋉V 的主丛上的协变场论，建立了拉格朗日与哈密顿两视角下的约化方案，推导出分解为 g-分量和 V-分量的约化 Euler-Poincaré 与 Lie-Poisson 场方程，并给出约化多辛形式、Noether 与能量动量平衡律；最终应用于双面矩阵微结构模型与二阶射流群，得到对称双曲系统及显式行波转换前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是解决结构群为中心半直积 G⋉V（而非标准半直积）的主丛上协变场论的约化问题，因为中心变量能保留双侧（左、右）几何数据，并通过 heart 与 diamond 算子分离左、右贡献，从而容纳标准半直积无法刻画的双面微结构等物理模型。

Method: 方法为：定义中心半直积 G⋉V 及其两侧交换线性表示，发展基于主丛的协变场论约化方案，从拉格朗日（Euler-Poincaré）与哈密顿（Lie-Poisson）两个视角导出分解为 g-分量与 V-分量的约化场方程，并推导约化多辛形式公式、Noether 定理与能量动量平衡律；所得结果与标准半直积情形的约化结果进行对比，利用 heart 与 diamond 算子分离左右贡献。随后将框架应用于双面矩阵微结构模型（3×3 矩阵经共轭表示分解为标量、矢量、对称无迹三个扇区）和二阶射流群 G_r^2。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 得到分解为 g-、V-分量的约化 Euler-Poincaré 场方程与 Lie-Poisson 场方程，以及约化多辛形式和 Noether/能量动量平衡律；(2) 矩阵模型中，共轭表示将 Mat(3) 分解为标量、矢量、对称无迹扇区，澄清了其与 director 及 alignment-tensor 变量的关系，且不与标准向列动力学混同；(3) 对 (1+1) 维 Minkowski 基底上的一维射流群，得到半线性对称双曲系统，并构造出线性与二次射流扇区之间显式能量动量交换的精确行波转换前沿；(4) 双曲目标解释给出线性化扰动的守恒正能量；(5) 非线性数值实验验证了约化演化及群值场的重构，并通过网格加密与独立波映射计算检验了一致性。

Conclusion: 结论是：中心半直积结构群为中心变量提供了一种自然约化框架，能保留双侧几何数据并分离左右贡献，优于标准半直积方案；该框架成功刻画双面矩阵微结构并揭示其与向列变量的区别，且在射流群情形下产生良性的对称双曲系统与可精确求解的转换前沿，数值实验进一步证实了约化与重构方案的可靠性。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a Lie group equipped with commuting left and right linear representations on a vector space $V$, and let $G\Join V$ be the associated centered semidirect product. We develop a reduction scheme for covariant field theories on principal bundles with structure group $G\Join V$ from both Lagrangian and Hamiltonian perspectives, obtaining the reduced Euler-Poincaré and Lie-Poisson field equations decomposed into their $\mathfrak g$- and $V$-components, where $\mathfrak g$ is the Lie algebra of $G$. We also derive the reduced multisymplectic form formula, as well as the Noether and energy-momentum balance laws. Our reduction results are compared with those for principal bundles with standard semidirect-product structure groups, and the role of the centered variables is investigated: they retain the original two-sided geometric data and separate the left and right contributions via the heart and diamond operators. The framework is then applied to a two-sided matrix microstructural model and to the second-order jet group on $\mathbb R^r$, $G_r^2$. For the matrix model, the conjugation representation decomposes $\operatorname{Mat}(3)$ into scalar, vector, and symmetric-traceless sectors, clarifying its precise relation to director and alignment-tensor variables without identifying it with standard nematodynamics. For the one-dimensional jet group on a $(1+1)$-dimensional Minkowski base, we obtain a semilinear symmetric-hyperbolic system and an exact traveling conversion front with explicit energy-momentum exchange between the linear- and quadratic-jet sectors. Its hyperbolic-target interpretation yields a conserved positive energy for the linearized perturbations. A nonlinear numerical experiment illustrates the reduced evolution and reconstruction of the group-valued field, with consistency assessed through mesh refinement and an independent wave-map computation.

</details>


### [109] [Dynamical quasi-symmetry of the classical Kepler-Coulomb problem and the conservation of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04650)
*Radosław Szmytkowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Kepler-Coulomb力作用下经典非相对论粒子的一种新的动力学准对称性，它由径向矢量的射影变换与时间微分的缩放共同构成，将牛顿运动方程映射为带符号函数的同类方程，并与已知的SO(N+1)/SO(N,1)动力学对称性互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Kepler-Coulomb问题寻找与已知SO(N+1)（负能量）与SO(N,1)（正能量）动力学对称性相互独立又互补的新的不变性，从而更完整地刻画该经典可积系统的对称结构。

Method: 构造扩展位形空间上的变换：径向矢量做射影变换 r→R=r/(1+c·r)，时间微分按 dt→dT=dt/(1+c·r)² 缩放，其中 c 为具有长度倒数量纲的实向量参数；证明该变换将方程 m(d²r/dt²)=αr/r³ 映射为 m(d²R/dT²)=(αR/R³)sgn(1−c·R)，并对其诱导的圆锥曲线轨迹映射、速度速端曲线映射进行解析与图形分析，最后由变换后运动的能量守恒推出原动力学中Laplace-Runge-Lenz矢量的守恒。

Result: 证明了该扩展位形空间变换构成动力学准对称性，它映射非简并圆锥轨迹之间的对应关系；该准对称性与SO(N+1)/SO(N,1)动力学对称性相互独立且互补；变换后运动的能量守恒等价于原系统中Laplace-Runge-Lenz矢量的守恒。

Conclusion: 所构造的射影变换加时间缩放的复合变换为Kepler-Coulomb问题提供了独立的动力学准对称性，揭示了变换系统的能量守恒与原系统Laplace-Runge-Lenz矢量守恒之间的内在联系，进一步完善了对该经典问题对称性结构的理解。

Abstract: The paper studies a classical non-relativistic particle moving in $\mathbb{R}^{N}$, $(N\geqslant2)$, in the presence of the Kepler-Coulomb force $\boldsymbol{F}(\boldsymbol{r})=α\boldsymbol{r}/r^{3}$, $(α\neq0)$. It is shown that the extended-configuration-space transformation involving the projective transformation of the radius vector $\boldsymbol{r}\to\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}/(1+\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{r})$ and the differential time scaling $\mathrm{d}t\to\mathrm{d}T=\mathrm{d}t/(1+\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{r})^{2}$, where $\boldsymbol{c}$ is a real vector-valued parameter with dimensions of inverse length, constitutes a dynamical quasi-symmetry in the sense that it maps Newton's equation of motion $m(\mathrm{d}^{2}\boldsymbol{r}/\mathrm{d}t^{2})=α\boldsymbol{r}/r^{3}$ into the equation $m(\mathrm{d}^{2}\boldsymbol{R}/\mathrm{d}T^{2})=(α\boldsymbol{R}/R^{3}) \operatorname{sgn}(1-\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{R})$. This quasi-symmetry is independent of, yet complementary to, the well-known Kepler-Coulomb dynamical symmetry, which is SO($N+1$) for negative energies and SO($N,1$) for positive energies. A detailed analytical and graphical analysis of induced mappings between non-degenerate conic trajectories associated with the original and transformed systems is presented. Mappings between velocity hodographs are also briefly discussed. It is then proved that the conservation of energy in the transformed motion implies the conservation of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector in the original dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Alternative Hamiltonian and Lagrangian Approaches for Constrained Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04692)
*M. A. de Andrade,C. Neves,E. V. Corrêa Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于修正的 Barcelos-Wotzasek (BW) 辛算法的替代方法，用于为受约束经典系统构建广义哈密顿量与拉格朗日框架，克服标准方法只适用于无约束系统的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的哈密顿量与拉格朗日方法虽对无约束经典系统十分有效，但无法直接处理受约束系统；尽管文献中已有成熟的处理方法，作者仍希望提供一种新的、替代性的推导框架。

Method: 基于修正的 Barcelos-Wotzasek (BW) 辛算法，推导广义哈密顿量和拉格朗日框架，为受约束系统构建一致的描述。

Result: 该方法能够一致且成功地构建受约束系统的标准拉格朗日量与哈密顿量描述。

Conclusion: 基于修正 BW 辛算法的替代方法可有效用于受约束系统，成功建立其拉格朗日与哈密顿形式的统一框架。

Abstract: While standard Hamiltonian and Lagrangian methods are powerful for unconstrained classical systems, they are not directly suited to constrained ones, despite the existence of well-established treatments in the literature. In this paper, we present an alternative approach to deriving a generalized Hamiltonian and Lagrangian framework, based on the revised Barcelos-Wotzasek (BW) symplectic algorithm. This method enables a consistent and successful construction of the standard Lagrangian and Hamiltonian descriptions for constrained systems.

</details>


### [111] [A bulk--edge correspondence for two-dimensional self-adjoint boundary and interface problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05616)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维拓扑绝缘体半平面边界（或两绝缘体界面）上非对称输运的量子化问题。关键发现是：与欧氏平面上的问题不同，截断（termination）样本需要选择自伴实现 H_L，而量子化输运确实依赖于这一选择。文章证明边/界面电流可观测量 σ_I[H_L]=Tr i[H_L,P]φ'(H_L) 沿两条路线良定义：对 Shapiro–Lopatinski 椭圆实现运用 Boutet de Monvel 演算证明其为良定义迹、I=2πσ_I 为 Fredholm 指标且在形变与紧支撑扰动下稳定；对沿边平移不变算子通过谱分析定义 I 为谱流与 Maslov 指标。主定理给出体-边对应公式 I=R−R_L+η(L_∞,L^Φ(+))−η(L_∞,L^Φ(−))，确定 I 的取值范围，并将公式推广到任意传输条件的界面，最后用八个例子验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欧氏平面（无边界）上，拓扑绝缘体的量子化非对称输运已由体指标刻画。但真实材料必然有边界或被截断，而截断需要选定自伴实现 H_L。作者旨在厘清：边界条件下量子化边电流是否仍完全由体指标决定，还是依赖于自伴实现的选取，并给出严格的体-边对应关系。

Method: 作者沿两条路线建立边/界面电流可观测量 σ_I[H_L]=Tr i[H_L,P]φ'(H_L) 的良定义性：(1) 对 Shapiro–Lopatinski 椭圆实现，利用 Boutet de Monvel 微局部演算中的迹估计证明该量为良定义迹，并说明 I=2πσ_I 是 Fredholm 指标，在算子形变与紧支撑扰动下稳定；(2) 对沿边平移不变算子，通过谱分析在大动量处边谱逸出体带隙时定义 I，此时 I 即为谱流与 Maslov 指标。主定理通过 η-不变量（eta invariant）的边界修正项建立体-边对应，并将其推广到任意传输条件的界面。

Result: 主定理给出体-边对应公式：I = R − R_L + η(L_∞, L^Φ(+)) − η(L_∞, L^Φ(−))，其中 R 为体指标、R_L 为边界（自伴实现）项、后两项为 η-不变量修正。作者确定了 I 的取值范围；在透明传输条件下，该不变量退化为欧氏平面的体差不变量；在任意传输条件的界面处公式同样成立。文章以八个例子展示了不同自伴实现下的不同量子化结果。

Conclusion: 拓扑绝缘体被截断为半平面（或构成界面）时，边界上的量子化非对称输运不能仅由体指标唯一确定，而是依赖于自伴实现 H_L 的选取。体-边对应需引入 η-不变量等边界修正项方能精确刻画。该框架统一了椭圆实现与平移不变情形，并推广至任意界面传输条件，揭示了边界条件对拓扑输运量子化的实质影响。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the quantized asymmetric transport along the boundary of a two-dimensional topological insulator occupying a half plane, or along the interface separating two insulators. In contrast with problems posed on the Euclidean plane, terminating a sample requires a choice of self-adjointrealization, $H_L$, and we show that a quantized transport depends on that choice.
  The physical observable modeling the asymmetric transport is the edge, or interface, current $σ_I[H_L]=\Tr\,i[H_L,P]\varphi'(H_L)$. It is shown to be well defined along two routes. For Shapiro--Lopatinski elliptic realizations, we use estimates from Boutet de Monvel's calculus to show that the current observable is a well-defined trace, that $\mathcal I=2πσ_I[H_L]$ is a Fredholm index, and that it is stable under deformations and compactly supported changes of the operator and of its termination. For translation invariant operators along the edge, a spectral analysis defines $\mathcal I$ when the edge spectrum escapes a bulk gap at large momenta. $\mathcal I$ is then also a spectral flow and a Maslov index.
  Our main theorem is a bulk--edge correspondence \[
  \mathcal I=\cR-\cR_L+η\bigl(L_\infty,\Lm^Φ(+)\bigr)-η\bigl(L_\infty,\Lm^Φ(-)\bigr), \] We determine the range of $\mathcal I$. We extend the formula to interfaces with arbitrary transmission conditions. At the transparent condition, the invariant equals the bulk-difference invariant of the Euclidean plane. Eight examples illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [112] [On the signaling dimension of the cross-polytope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05924)
*Tamás Bencze,Péter E. \ Frenkel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一般概率信息论框架下交叉多胞体（cross-polytopes）的信令维度，已知3、4、5维交叉多胞体的信令维度为3，作者为更高维交叉多胞体的信令维度给出了上界和下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信令维度刻画了经典通信协议模拟给定协议所需的最小复杂度。此前已知3、4、5维交叉多胞体的信令维度均为3，但对于更高维交叉多胞体的信令维度尚无明确结果，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 在一般概率信息论模型中，将通信协议等价于其状态空间，通过构造经典通信协议来模拟给定协议的输入-输出关联，从而界定信令维度。作者分别构造上界（显式模拟协议）和下界（基于适当不变量的下界论证），给出高维交叉多胞体的信令维度范围。

Result: 作者为更高维交叉多胞体的信令维度给出了上界和下界，从而将其信令维度限定在一定的数值区间内，推广了此前低维（3、4、5维）信令维度为3的已知结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功确定了高维交叉多胞体信令维度的上下界，推进了对一般概率模型中信令维度这一信息论不变量的理解，并为后续进一步精确确定其值奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the general probabilistic model of information theory, the signaling dimension of a given communication protocol (or, equivalently, of its state space) is the smallest possible complexity of a classical communication protocol capable of perfectly simulating the given one in terms of input-output correlations.
  The signaling dimensions of three-, four-, and five-dimensional cross-polytopes have been known to be 3. We give an upper and a lower bound for signaling dimensions of higher dimensional cross-polytopes.

</details>


### [113] [Asymptotically free solutions of the scalar flow equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06165)
*Christoph Kopper*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了四维欧氏空间中标量场论重整化群流方程在非多项式边界条件下，存在具有UV渐近自由、Bose对称、欧氏群不变、对流参数与动量解析且满足Osterwalder-Schrader增长条件的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量场论中n点Schwinger函数的重整化群流方程，探索非多项式边界条件下是否存在满足物理要求（对称性、解析性、OS公理条件）的UV渐近自由解，从而拓展对可构造量子场论的理解。

Method: 分析四维欧氏空间中具有非多项式边界条件的n点Schwinger函数重整化群流方程的解，验证其Bose对称性、欧氏群不变性、对流参数和动量的解析性，以及Osterwalder-Schrader增长条件的满足情况。

Result: 证明了存在一类UV渐近自由解，这些解具备Bose对称性、欧氏群不变性，对流动参数和动量解析，并满足Osterwalder-Schrader增长条件。

Conclusion: 非多项式边界条件下，四维欧氏标量场论的重整化群流方程存在满足全部所需物理性质的UV渐近自由解，表明这类构造在数学上与物理上均是一致的。

Abstract: We analyse solutions of the flow equations of the renormalisation group for the n-point Schwinger functions of scalar field theory in four-dimensional Euclidean space with nonpolynomial boundary conditions. We show that there exist UV asymptotically free solutions of this type which are Bose symmetric, invariant under the Euclidean group, analytic in the flow parameter and the momenta, and satisfy the Osterwalder-Schrader growth condition.

</details>


### [114] [Entropy Contraction and Hypercontractivity for Gaussian Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06201)
*Zhengwei Liu,Jincheng Wan,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有忠实不变高斯态的有限模高斯量子马尔可夫半群，给出了相对于固定点代数的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式的代数刻画及最优常数，并建立了Hurwitz漂移下的超压缩性与逆向超压缩性结果，以及p-对数Sobolev不等式的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数Sobolev型不等式（尤其完全修正的对数Sobolev不等式）在量子信息与量子Markov半群的混合速度、超压缩性等问题中至关重要。此前对高斯量子Markov半群在固定点代数上的完全CLSI缺乏系统刻画，最优常数与超压缩/逆向超压缩的精确时间尺度亦未完全解决，且p-对数Sobolev常数的符号性质（如p<1/2时为负）未被阐明。

Method: 作者聚焦具有忠实不变高斯态的有限模高斯量子Markov半群，采用代数方法刻画相对于固定点代数的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式（CLSI）；在漂移演化 e^{tZ} 收敛（t→∞）的假设下求取最优常数；利用Hurwitz漂移条件建立超压缩性及逆向超压缩性；并系统研究p-对数Sobolev不等式。

Result: 给出了完全CLSI相对于固定点代数的代数刻画，并在漂移演化收敛假设下得到最优常数；证明Hurwitz漂移下任意固定对 1<p<q<∞ 在充分大时间处可被达到（超压缩性）；发现通常的从零时刻起具正速率的逆向超压缩曲线一般可能失败，但在Hurwitz假设下任意固定对 1/2<p<q<1 在充分大时间处可达到；并证明当 0<p<1/2 时p-对数Sobolev常数可为负。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了高斯量子Markov半群在固定点代数上的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式及其最优常数，澄清了超压缩性与逆向超压缩性的可达到性条件与时间尺度，并揭示了小参数范围（0<p<1/2）下p-对数Sobolev常数为负的新现象，深化了对量子Markov半群泛函不等式的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study finite-mode Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful invariant Gaussian state. We present an algebraic characterization of the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality relative to the fixed-point algebra, and we obtain the corresponding optimal constant under the assumption that the drift evolution $e^{t\mathbf{Z}}$ converges as $t\to\infty$. We establish hypercontractivity for such quantum Markov semigroups with Hurwitz drift, in the sense that every fixed pair $1<p<q<\infty$ is attained at sufficiently large times. We find that the usual reverse hypercontractive curves with a positive rate from time zero can fail in general, but every fixed pair $1/2 < p < q < 1$ is attained at sufficiently large times under the Hurwitz assumption. We also systematically investigate the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev inequality and show that the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant can be negative for $0 < p < 1/2$.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Fluctuations in Bose--Einstein Condensates:Microscopic Correlations and Moment Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06367)
*Mauris Chueng,Keheng Zhu,Hexiang Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维Gross–Pitaevskii区域中量子涨落的高阶矩与微观关联能，传播第三谱矩并给出激发能测度在N²尺度的极限，证明相对散射算子的谱测度乘以时间依赖的dressed contact，并给出有限立方近似的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究玻色气体在Gross–Pitaevskii区域中量子涨落的高阶矩与微观关联能，以刻画超越平均场（凝聚体）描述的量子关联效应，并确定激发能测度的精确极限行为。

Method: 针对光滑、紧支撑、非负径向相互作用以及H^6中的N无关归一化初始凝聚波函数，沿精确多体演化传播正关联能的第三谱矩；借助一致初始界控制耗散数三阶矩与集体单粒子涨落六阶矩；对非零相互作用与光滑紧支撑初始凝聚，在N²尺度识别由第一能量加权的激发能测度，并将其正能部分表示为相对散射算子-2Δ+v的谱测度乘以时间依赖的dressed contact；利用正初始能量的逆幂构造容许的纯态与混合态族，并建立有限立方近似。

Result: 一致初始界在任意紧时间区间上独立于N控制耗散数三阶矩和集体单粒子涨落六阶矩；对范数收敛纯态族，激发能测度在N²尺度的极限成立，其正能部分为相对散射算子谱测度乘时间依赖dressed contact；径向密度可计算且contact对非零参考态严格为正；演化态具有有界物理涨落矩，p>1阶激发能矩在扩展谱意义下被N^{2p-2}的正倍数下界控制；对范数收敛纯态类确定1≤p<2的尺度化相互作用能矩并以O(N²)界住二阶矩；有限立方近似在能量形式域上范数误差O(N^{-3/16})，在生成元更强初始域上为O(N^{-5/4})。

Conclusion: 在三维Gross–Pitaevskii区域中，量子涨落高阶矩与微观关联能可被严格刻画：第三谱矩沿多体演化一致受控，激发能测度在N²尺度收敛于相对散射算子谱测度与dressed contact的乘积，contact严格为正，且有限立方近似具有明确的范数误差界。

Abstract: We study higher moments of quantum fluctuations and microscopic correlation energy in the three-dimensional Gross--Pitaevskii regime. For a smooth, compactly supported, nonnegative radial interaction and an $N$-independent normalized initial condensate wave function in $H^6$, we propagate the third spectral moment of a positive correlated energy along the exact many-body evolution. A uniform initial bound controls the third moment of the depletion number and sixth moments of collective one-particle fluctuations on every compact time interval, independently of $N$. For a nonzero interaction and a smooth, compactly supported initial condensate, we also identify the excitation-energy measure at scale $N^2$, weighted by the first energy. This limit holds for norm-convergent pure-state families with a uniform bound on the initial third correlated-energy moment. Its positive-energy part is a spectral measure of the relative scattering operator $-2Δ+v$, multiplied by a time-dependent dressed contact. We compute the radial density and prove strict positivity of the contact for nonzero references with finite kinetic energy and finite mean excitation number. The same evolving states have bounded physical fluctuation moments and excitation-energy moments of order $p>1$ bounded below by a positive multiple of $N^{2p-2}$, in the extended spectral sense. Inverse powers of the positive initial energy construct admissible pure and mixed families. For the norm-convergent pure-state class, we determine the scaled interaction-energy moments for $1\le p<2$ and bound the second moment by $O(N^2)$. A finite cubic approximation has norm error $O(N^{-3/16})$ on the energy form domain and $O(N^{-5/4})$ on a stronger initial domain of its generator.

</details>


### [116] [Electrostatic inequalities and stability of matter on asymptotically Euclidean manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06624)
*David Stuart*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在渐近欧几里得黎曼流形上，证明了存在固定原子核时非相对论费米子物质的稳定性，仅需截面曲率有上下界而无符号条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子多体系统稳定性的经典结果（Lieb-Thirring 不等式与静电不等式）从欧几里得空间推广到弯曲的渐近欧几里得黎曼流形，并希望不依赖曲率的符号条件，从而得到更普适的稳定性判据。

Method: 证明沿用 Lieb-Thirring 不等式与静电不等式的路线；其中静电不等式通过一种基于 Green 函数 {G(·,R_j)} 而非距离函数的 Voronoi 分解型论证来建立，并假设截面曲率具有上下界。

Result: 在仅假设截面曲率有上下界、不对曲率符号作任何限制的条件下，证明了存在固定原子核 {R_j} 时非相对论费米子物质在渐近欧几里得黎曼流形 (M,g) 上的稳定性。

Conclusion: 无需曲率符号条件的稳定性结果得以建立，关键创新在于用 Green 函数替代距离函数构造 Voronoi 分解，从而在弯曲流形上有效处理静电不等式。

Abstract: We prove stability of nonrelativistic fermionic matter in the presence of fixed nuclei \(\{R_j\}\) on an
  asymptotically Euclidean Riemannian manifold \((M,g)\).
  Upper and lower bounds on sectional curvatures are assumed, but
  no specific sign conditions on the curvature are imposed.
  The proof follows the route of Lieb-Thirring and electrostatic inequalities, the latter being
  proved by means of a Voronoi decomposition type argument which is however based on the Green functions \(\{G(\,\cdot\,,R_j)\}\) rather than the distance function.

</details>


### [117] [A Cohomological Characterization of the Clifford Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06699)
*Junaid Aftab*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用非阿贝尔 1-上循环（1-cocycle）框架，给出 Clifford 层级（Clifford hierarchy）的递归上同调刻画，并借此证明了第三层级中所有二量子门与三量子门都是 semi-Clifford 门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford 层级在容错量子计算中处于核心地位，但其高层级结构尚未被完全理解。已有基于量子高阶傅里叶分析的工作通过量子导数刻画层级成员资格，却未给出各层级算子结构的显式描述。本文旨在弥补这一空白，为 Clifford 层级提供显式的、逐层的代数刻画。

Method: 作者证明酉算子的量子导数集合构成一个非阿贝尔 1-上循环，并且反过来每个这样的 1-上循环都由某个酉算子产生，从而得到 Clifford 层级的递归上同调刻画。随后将该框架专门应用于第三层级，把上循环数据分解为辛（symplectic）、仿射（affine）与相位（phase）三个分量，每一分量都具有上同调解释。

Result: 利用上述分解框架，作者给出了一个新证明：第三层级 Clifford 层级中的每个二量子比特门（two-qudit gate）都是 semi-Clifford 门；并首次证明了第三层级中的每个三量子比特门（three-qudit gate）也都是 semi-Clifford 门。

Conclusion: 通过将量子导数解释为非阿贝尔 1-上循环并逐层分解，本文为 Clifford 层级提供了递归的上同调刻画，并成功将其应用于第三层级，把 semi-Clifford 性质的证明从二量子门推广到三量子门。

Abstract: The Clifford hierarchy plays a central role in fault-tolerant quantum computation, but its higher levels remain only partially understood. Recent work on quantum higher order Fourier analysis characterizes membership in the Clifford hierarchy using quantum derivatives, but does not provide an explicit description of the individual levels. In this work, we address this problem by showing that the collection of quantum derivatives of a unitary operator forms a non-abelian \(1\)-cocycle, and conversely that every such \(1\)-cocycle arises from a unitary operator. This yields a recursive cohomological characterization of the Clifford hierarchy. We then specialize this framework to the third level and decompose the cocycle data into symplectic, affine, and phase components, each admitting a cohomological interpretation. As applications, we give a new proof that every two-qudit gate in the third level of the Clifford hierarchy is semi-Clifford, and prove for the first time that every three-qudit gate in the third level is semi-Clifford.

</details>
